TASK [Stop and disable unattended-upgrades and apt-daily (prevents apt lock)] ***********************************************************************************************************************************
changed: [Host34_1] =>
    changed: true
    cmd: |-
        systemctl stop unattended-upgrades.service 2>/dev/null || true
        systemctl disable unattended-upgrades.service 2>/dev/null || true
        systemctl stop apt-daily.timer apt-daily-upgrade.timer 2>/dev/null || true
        systemctl disable apt-daily.timer apt-daily-upgrade.timer 2>/dev/null || true
        systemctl stop apt-daily.service apt-daily-upgrade.service 2>/dev/null || true
        systemctl kill --signal=SIGTERM apt-daily.service 2>/dev/null || true
        # Wait for any running apt/dpkg process to finish (max 60s)
        for i in $(seq 1 60); do
          if fuser /var/lib/apt/lists/lock /var/lib/dpkg/lock-frontend /var/lib/dpkg/lock 2>/dev/null; then
            sleep 1
          else
            break
          fi
        done
        # Remove stale lock files only if no process holds them
        if ! fuser /var/lib/apt/lists/lock 2>/dev/null; then
          rm -f /var/lib/apt/lists/lock
        fi
        if ! fuser /var/lib/dpkg/lock-frontend 2>/dev/null; then
          rm -f /var/lib/dpkg/lock-frontend
        fi
        if ! fuser /var/lib/dpkg/lock 2>/dev/null; then
          rm -f /var/lib/dpkg/lock
        fi
    delta: '0:00:02.620826'
    end: '2026-06-06 22:58:12.804486'

    rc: 0

    stdout_lines: <omitted>

TASK [Ensure default gateway is set (KVM nodes have no gateway by default)] *************************************************************************************************************************************
ok: [Host34_1] =>
    changed: false
    cmd: |-
        GATEWAY="172.16.2.1"
        IFACE=$(ip -o -4 route show to "${GATEWAY%.*}.0/24" 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $3}' | head -1)
        if [ -z "$IFACE" ]; then
          IFACE=$(ip -o link show up | grep -v lo | awk -F': ' '{print $2}' | head -1)
        fi
        # Check if default route already exists
        if ip route show default | grep -q "via $GATEWAY"; then
          echo "OK: default route already set via $GATEWAY"
          exit 0
        fi
        # Add temporary default route
        ip route add default via "$GATEWAY" dev "$IFACE" 2>/dev/null ||  ip route replace default via "$GATEWAY" dev "$IFACE"
        echo "ADDED: default via $GATEWAY dev $IFACE"
    delta: '0:00:00.015795'
    end: '2026-06-06 22:59:21.760411'
    msg: ''
    rc: 0
    start: '2026-06-06 22:59:21.744616'
    stderr: ''

    stdout: 'OK: default route already set via 172.16.2.1'
    stdout_lines: <omitted>

TASK [Make default gateway permanent via netplan] ***************************************************************************************************************************************************************
ok: [Host12_1] =>
    changed: false
    cmd: |-
        GATEWAY="172.16.2.1"
        NETPLAN_FILE=$(ls /etc/netplan/*.yaml 2>/dev/null | head -1)
        if [ -z "$NETPLAN_FILE" ]; then
          echo "SKIP: no netplan file found"
          exit 0
        fi
        if grep -q "via: *${GATEWAY}" "$NETPLAN_FILE" 2>/dev/null; then
          echo "OK: gateway already in netplan"
          exit 0
        fi
        # Get the primary interface and its IP
        IFACE=$(ip -o -4 route show to "${GATEWAY%.*}.0/24" 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $3}' | head -1)
        IP=$(ip -o -4 addr show dev "$IFACE" 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $4}' | head -1)
        if [ -z "$IFACE" ] || [ -z "$IP" ]; then
          echo "SKIP: could not determine interface or IP"
          exit 0
        fi
        # Write a clean netplan config with gateway and DNS
        cat > "$NETPLAN_FILE" <<EOF
        network:
          version: 2
          ethernets:
            ${IFACE}:
              addresses:
                - ${IP}
              routes:
                - to: default
                  via: ${GATEWAY}
              nameservers:
                addresses:
                  - 8.8.8.8
                  - 1.1.1.1
        EOF
        # Fix indentation (remove leading 8 spaces from heredoc)
        sed -i 's/^        //' "$NETPLAN_FILE"
        chmod 600 "$NETPLAN_FILE"
        netplan apply 2>/dev/null || true
        echo "DONE: netplan updated with gateway $GATEWAY on $IFACE ($IP)"
    delta: '0:00:00.013010'
    end: '2026-06-06 22:59:26.076939'
    msg: ''
    rc: 0
    start: '2026-06-06 22:59:26.063929'
    stderr: ''
    stderr_lines: <omitted>
    stdout: 'OK: gateway already in netplan'
    stdout_lines: <omitted>

TASK [Patch KubeSpray: Skip apt cache update (packages pre-installed in Phase 3)] *******************************************************************************************************************************
ok: [localhost] =>
    changed: false
    cmd: |-
        set -e
        TARGET=$(grep -rl "Update package management cache (APT)"  /opt/fabric-cache/kubespray/src/roles/kubernetes/preinstall/tasks/ 2>/dev/null | head -1)
        if [ -z "$TARGET" ]; then
          echo "SKIP: task file not found"
          exit 0
        fi
        if grep -q "PATCHED_SKIP_APT_UPDATE" "$TARGET"; then
          echo "SKIP: already patched"
          exit 0
        fi
        # The actual kubespray task looks like:
        #   - name: Update package management cache (APT)
        #     apt:
        #       update_cache: yes
        #       cache_valid_time: 3600
        #     when: ansible_os_family == "Debian"
        #
        # Replace the existing 'when:' line with 'when: false'
        python3 -c "
        import re, sys
        target = sys.argv[1]
        with open(target, 'r') as f:
            content = f.read()
        patched = re.sub(
            r'(- name: Update package management cache \(APT\)\n'
            r'  apt:\n'
            r'    update_cache: (?:yes|true)\n'
            r'    cache_valid_time: \d+\n)'
            r'  when: .*',
            r'\g<1>  when: false  # PATCHED_SKIP_APT_UPDATE — air-gapped, packages pre-installed',
            content
        )
        if patched != content:
            with open(target, 'w') as f:
                f.write(patched)
            print('DONE: patched ' + target)
        else:
            print('SKIP: pattern not matched in ' + target)
        " "$TARGET"
    delta: '0:00:00.016371'
    end: '2026-06-06 18:00:28.622308'
    msg: ''
    rc: 0
    start: '2026-06-06 18:00:28.605937'
    stderr: ''
    stderr_lines: <omitted>
    stdout: 'SKIP: already patched'
    stdout_lines: <omitted>

        patched = re.sub(

            # Fallback: simpler pattern — just add when: false after the name line

                r'(- name: Install packages requirements\n)',
                r'\g<1>  when: false  # PATCHED_SKIP_PKG_INSTALL — air-gapped, packages pre-installed in Phase 3\n',
                content
            )
        if patched != content:
            with open(target, 'w') as f:
                f.write(patched)
            print('DONE: patched ' + target)
        else:
            print('SKIP: pattern not matched in ' + target)
        " "$TARGET"
